# Mallan platform — reconciliation ledger

> **Complete inventory: 608 rows. Resolved so far: 608. Still unresolved: 0.**
>
> A row is `unresolved` until it appears in `RECONCILIATION-RESOLUTIONS.json`.
> **Silence is never read as a decision** — an unresolved row means no
> reconciliation judgement has been made about it, not that it was accepted.
> A superficially complete ledger with invented dispositions would look like
> accounting while hiding exactly that.
>
> Rows marked `code_verified` had their claim about the current codebase checked
> by reading the cited `file:line` at commit `04db1b99` (safe `main`), read-only.
> Rows marked `source_read` are policy statements with nothing in code to verify.

Gates 1 (Owner Opt-Out) and 2 (Participant Only) fail closed absolutely. Gates 3 (InternetEntireListingDisplayYN) and 4 (InternetAddressDisplayYN): NULL MEANS DISPLAYABLE — block only on explicit false, do NOT require affirmation. Gates 5 (AVM) and 6 (Consumer Comment): null means BLOCKED — require affirmation, null denies. Gates 5 and 6 are per-row opt-out flags the provider DOES populate at row level, so null there legitimately means 'not set' and must deny. Retaining the rationale is what makes the rule defensible rather than memorized. | POL-1.1 | decided | not_started | source_read |

Same shape as docs/PLATFORM-ISSUE-REGISTRY.md, where OPS-024 and OPS-025 already live. | MAIN-GOVERNANCE-003, GATE-1 | decided | partially_implemented | source_read |
| ERR-6 | recovered-plan | 6e8ea2d9 | ## ERR-6 | No hanging catches or warnings | §Errors — no hanging catches or warnings | retained | Temporary catches, fallback branches, warnings, debug logs and error comments require a disposition and a removal condition and may not remain indefinitely without ownership. | ERR-7 | recovered-plan | 6e8ea2d9 | ## ERR-7 | Provider failure is never a false empty | §Errors — provider failure is never a false empty | retained | A provider error, timeout, throttle or authentication failure returns PROVIDER_UNAVAILABLE and NEVER an empty success, because 'an empty result asserts that the provider was reached and matched nothing'. That sentence states precisely what an empty result MEANS and should transfer verbatim. Compliance-relevant: a Cotality outage silently rendering as 'no listings match' misrepresents inventory to the public. | ERR-1, ERR-2, COT-13, SEA-8 | decided | not_started | source_read |
| AUD-1 | recovered-plan | 6e8ea2d9 | ## AUD-1 | Baseline | §Audit — baseline | retained | The current count of conforming entry points is recorded once as a baseline, so conformance is measured against a real starting point rather than an aspiration. | AUD-2 | decided | not_started | source_read |
| AUD-2 | recovered-plan | 6e8ea2d9 | ## AUD-2 | Ratchet | §Audit — ratchet | retained | The validator fails if conformance DECREASES. New entry points must conform; existing non-conforming ones migrate at any pace but are never added to. A ratchet permits incremental progress without permitting regression — the same shape as the ucba:audit REGRESSIONS=0 hard stop in CLAUDE.md §G. | AUD-1, GATE-7 | decided | not_started | source_read |
| AUD-3 | recovered-plan | 6e8ea2d9 | ## AUD-3 | What the audit checks | §Audit — what the audit checks | retained | Eight checks: entry-point classification, transport-exception registration, envelope conformance, explicit result state on collection responses, error-catalog conformance, generated-client drift, application-service boundary per ARC-9, and provider-vocabulary drift per COT-12. | AUD-4 | recovered-plan | 6e8ea2d9 | ## AUD-4 | The audit states its limits | §Audit — the audit states its limits | retained | The validator must state what it PROVES and what it DOES NOT: it verifies contract conformance; it does not verify correctness, authorization behavior or live operation. Identical in spirit to CLAUDE.md §J.8 ('no green-checks claim stands alone'). This ledger applies the same standard to itself via its verification_status column. | GATE-6, CLAUDE.md §J.8 | decided | not_started | source_read |
| AUD-5 | recovered-plan | 6e8ea2d9 | ## AUD-5 | Audit failures block | §Audit — audit failures block | retained | An audit failure BLOCKS the change; it is not advisory output. Advisory validators are ignored under deadline pressure, which is the failure mode this row exists to prevent. | AUD-3, GATE-8 | decided | not_started | source_read |
| HYG-2 | recovered-plan | 6e8ea2d9 | ## HYG-2 | What counts as bloat | §Hygiene — what counts as bloat | retained | Sixteen bloat categories including duplicate architecture documents (the condition this reconciliation exists to end), duplicate media or listing rows without reconciliation, and copied provider facts that become conflicting local truth. Closes with a protection that must survive: HISTORICAL MIGRATIONS ARE NOT BLOAT and must not be deleted merely to make the repository smaller. | HYG-3, DOC-4 | decided | not_started | source_read |
| HYG-3 | recovered-plan | 6e8ea2d9 | ## HYG-3 | Disposition states | §Hygiene — disposition states | retained | Eight cleanup states: KEEP_ACTIVE, MIGRATE, CONSOLIDATE, EVIDENCE_ONLY, HISTORICAL, DEPRECATE_AFTER_PROOF, DELETE_AFTER_PROOF, UNKNOWN_REQUIRES_TRACE. The two AFTER_PROOF states and UNKNOWN_REQUIRES_TRACE are what stop a cleanup pass from becoming a deletion pass. Structurally the same device as this ledger's disposition column, where `unresolved` plays the UNKNOWN_REQUIRES_TRACE role. | HYG-2, HYG-6 | decided | not_started | source_read |
| HYG-4 | recovered-plan | 6e8ea2d9 | ## HYG-4 | Required inventories | §Hygiene — required inventories | retained | Twelve machine-readable or GENERATED inventories: normative documents, routes and methods, non-HTTP entry points, errors, flags, environment variables, crons and jobs, provider adapters, database models and fields, public assets, dependencies, temporary compatibility code. | HYG-5 | recovered-plan | 6e8ea2d9 | ## HYG-5 | Temporary code contract | §Hygiene — temporary code contract | retained | Every temporary item records owner, reason, introduced_at, removal_condition, latest_review_date and a test proving safe removal. | HYG-6 | recovered-plan | 6e8ea2d9 | ## HYG-6 | Safe removal proof | §Hygiene — safe removal proof | retained | Eight ordered steps before removal. Step 2 is the load-bearing one — DISTINGUISH 'no caller found in searched paths' FROM 'unused' — and steps 3-5 require replacement behavior, proof of it, and preserved rollback BEFORE the old path is removed. This is the procedure that makes HYG-1 actionable rather than merely prohibitive. | HYG-1, OPS-5 | decided | not_started | source_read |
| HYG-7 | recovered-plan | 6e8ea2d9 | ## HYG-7 | Per-change hygiene questions | §Hygiene — per-change hygiene questions | retained | Seven questions every PR answers, ending with 'What proves no unrelated files were included?' — which is the check that makes HYG-9 and OPS-6 verifiable rather than aspirational. | HYG-9, OPS-6 | decided | not_started | source_read |

| SEA-7 | recovered-plan | 6e8ea2d9 | ## SEA-7 | Matched-pair suppression order | §Search — matched-pair suppression order | retained | CODE-VERIFIED DEFECT — the current DB path violates this ordering. SEA-7 requires identity reconciliation and public suppression BEFORE final totals, pagination, saved-search replay, sitemap output and similar-listing output. In app/api/listings/route.ts the order is inverted: pagination is applied inside the query (`skip: dbSkip, take: dbTake`, lines 332-337); the total comes from `prisma.listing.count({ where: dbWhere })` on the SAME where clause with no suppression (line 415); then filterDisplayableDbListings runs on the already-cut page (line 434) and preferCrmExclusiveOverIdxDuplicate runs after it (line 441). Consequences that follow structurally: the reported total overstates by every suppressed duplicate and non-displayable row across the full result set, and a page can return fewer than `limit` items — which SEA-10 criterion 3 ('pagination with no unreachable results') forbids. Line 1086 adjusts the total from page-local counts, which cannot correct a full-set overstatement. NOT verified: the live-Trestle fallback branch ordering, and whether any production query currently contains a matched pair spanning a page boundary. | LST-1, LST-7, SEA-10 | decided | partially_implemented | code_verified |
| SEA-8 | recovered-plan | 6e8ea2d9 | ## SEA-8 | Search states | §Search — search states | retained | Nine distinct states the interface must separate: loading, success, empty, unsupported, not authorized, stale, provider unavailable, partial/degraded, contract incompatible. The critical separations are empty-vs-failure and empty-vs-not-authorized; collapsing them is how a provider outage silently reads as 'no listings match'. | ERR-1, ERR-7, SEA-5 | decided | not_started | source_read |

Criterion 3 (pagination with no unreachable results) and criterion 15 (one matched pair produces one public result and one canonical Mallan URL) are both currently at risk from the SEA-7 ordering defect. | SEA-7, LST-1 | decided | not_started | code_verified |

| TXN-1 | recovered-plan | 6e8ea2d9 | ## TXN-1 | Mallan does not draft or review contracts | §Transactions — no contract drafting or review | retained | NY law prohibits brokers from drafting or reviewing contracts of sale; doing so is unauthorized practice of law and demonstrates untrustworthiness and incompetence under RPL §441-c. Authorities cited: NY DOS Legal Memorandum LI04, Duncan & Hill Realty v. Department of State, Judiciary Law §484, RPL §441-c. Five prohibited system behaviors enumerated, closing with the catch-all that any feature producing contract language for a live transaction is prohibited REGARDLESS OF HOW IT IS LABELLED — which is the clause that survives a future 'AI clause assistant' proposal. | TXN-11, BIZ-0 (authority is NY_STATUTE) | decided | not_started | source_read |
| TXN-2 | recovered-plan | 6e8ea2d9 | ## TXN-2 | Mallan does not hold escrow or any transaction funds | §Transactions — no escrow or transaction funds | retained | The buyer wires the deposit (customarily 10%) to the SELLER'S ATTORNEY'S escrow account; Mallan never receives, holds, controls, disburses or reconciles transaction funds. Five prohibitions including modelling an escrow balance and generating wire instructions. The permitted carve-out is precise and must survive: the system MAY record, as reported facts from the attorneys, that a deposit was made, its date, and the escrow holder's identity — milestone facts, never a Mallan-held balance. | TXN-11, BRK-6 | decided | not_started | source_read |
| TXN-3 | recovered-plan | 6e8ea2d9 | ## TXN-3 | The deal sheet is the brokerage artifact | §Transactions — the deal sheet | retained | The deal sheet is the brokerage artifact — a summary of agreed terms transmitted to the attorneys, explicitly NOT a contract and NOT binding. Fifteen minimum fields enumerated. Every deal sheet must carry a visible statement that it is not a contract, is not binding, and that the parties should be represented by counsel. That visible statement is what keeps the artifact on the correct side of TXN-1. | TXN-1, TXN-6, TXN-12 | decided | not_started | source_read |
| TXN-4 | recovered-plan | 6e8ea2d9 | ## TXN-4 | Transaction state is observed, not owned | §Transactions — state is observed, not owned | retained | After the deal sheet the transaction is driven by attorneys, lenders, boards and managing agents; Mallan observes and coordinates but does not control state. Every milestone records reported_by, reported_at, source_evidence and confidence — a REPORTED FACT WITH A SOURCE, never a system-asserted truth. ARC-3 applies: an unknown milestone state is explicit, never assumed complete. Directly parallels PER-9's refusal to promote an inference into a fact. | ARC-3, PER-9, TXN-5 | decided | not_started | source_read |
| TXN-5 | recovered-plan | 6e8ea2d9 | ## TXN-5 | Sale milestone sequence | §Transactions — sale milestone sequence | retained | Seventeen-step NYC sale sequence from offer submitted through commission earned, with 'contract countersigned by seller' explicitly marked as the point it becomes binding. Customary timelines (co-op ~60-90 days contract-to-closing, condo ~30-45) are recorded as SCHEDULING EXPECTATIONS, explicitly not guarantees and not encoded as deadlines asserting legal consequence — a distinction that must survive into any alerting feature. | TXN-6, TXN-7, BIZ-6 | decided | not_started | source_read |
| TXN-6 | recovered-plan | 6e8ea2d9 | ## TXN-6 | Acceptance is not binding | §Transactions — acceptance is not binding | retained | An accepted offer creates NO binding obligation in this market; the transaction becomes binding on full execution (buyer signs, seller countersigns). The system must never describe an accepted offer as a sale, commitment, binding agreement or closed transaction in ANY interface, report, seller communication, marketing surface or metric. The 'or metric' clause is the one most easily violated — a pipeline dashboard counting accepted offers as closed volume breaches this. | TXN-5, BIZ-6, BRK-7 | decided | not_started | source_read |
| TXN-7 | recovered-plan | 6e8ea2d9 | ## TXN-7 | Board approval is the principal deal risk | §Transactions — board approval as principal risk | retained | Co-op board approval is the largest single source of transaction failure in this market and is modelled as a first-class risk, not a checklist row: package requirements, assembly status, submission date, review status, interview scheduling, decision, and conditions on a conditional approval. The deposit-recovery consequence is stated as GENERAL MARKET PRACTICE and never as advice on a specific transaction, because the governing terms are in the contract Mallan does not draft or interpret (TXN-1). | TXN-1, TXN-8, TXN-5 | decided | not_started | source_read |
| TXN-8 | recovered-plan | 6e8ea2d9 | ## TXN-8 | Board package assistance has a boundary | §Transactions — board package boundary | retained | The brokerage may support checklist tracking, document collection, completeness review against the building's stated requirements, and submission logistics. It must NOT generate financial statements, characterize a client's financial position, draft reference letters as though authored by the referee, or produce any representation to a board that did not originate with its actual author. The reference-letter prohibition is an authorship-integrity rule that directly constrains AI generation features. | TXN-7, TXN-1, PER-9 | decided | not_started | source_read |
| TXN-9 | recovered-plan | 6e8ea2d9 | ## TXN-9 | Rental transactions | §Transactions — rental transactions | retained | Distinct rental sequence, with four verified market/legal parameters carrying an explicit authority distinction that must not be lost: the 40x income rule is a WIDESPREAD MARKET CONVENTION, NOT A STATUTE, and must be described as a landlord requirement rather than a legal one — exactly the BIZ-0 misattribution failure mode. Guarantor convention ~80x and NY/NJ/CT residency; application fees capped at $20 with limited co-op/condo exceptions; upfront costs capped at first month plus one month security. | BIZ-0, TXN-10, BIZ-12 | decided | not_started | source_read |
| TXN-10 | recovered-plan | 6e8ea2d9 | ## TXN-10 | FARE Act fee attribution | §Transactions — FARE Act fee attribution | retained | CODE-VERIFIED PARTIAL — the WRITE gate is real and fail-closed. NYC LL 119/2024: the party who hires the broker pays. app/api/crm/listings/[id]/route.ts:186-203 and app/api/crm/listings/[id]/status/route.ts:137-146 both block a rental from becoming display-ready without fee disclosure, returning code FARE_FEE_DISCLOSURE on field MoveInCostsAmount; app/components/FareActFeeBadge.tsx exists for rendering; app/api/idx/search/route.ts:68 handles rental fee transparency. NOT VERIFIED IN THIS SESSION: that the disclosure actually RENDERS on public rental listing pages. CLAUDE.md §F records a 2026-05-20 audit where the FARE source-grep passed while the conditional did not render on production rentals — a $1,800-2,000-per-violation exposure. Verifying the render requires a live URL probe, which was not run here. Left partially_implemented for that reason and NOT claimed as satisfied. | POL-1 (fails closed), BIZ-12, live-probe verification outstanding | decided | partially_implemented | code_verified |
| TXN-11 | recovered-plan | 6e8ea2d9 | ## TXN-11 | Prohibited surfaces | §Transactions — prohibited surfaces | retained | Eight surfaces that must not be built, generated or presented in ANY phase: escrow ledger or trust accounting, deposit custody naming Mallan as holder, contract drafting/assembly/templating, contract review/annotation/clause interpretation, rider or amendment generation, legal advice including any statement of what a party is entitled to under a contract, wire instructions, title insurance issuance or binding. The correct behavior where a client needs any of these is to RECORD THE NEED AND ROUTE IT TO COUNSEL — a positive instruction, not just a prohibition. | TXN-1, TXN-2, TXN-12 | decided | not_started | source_read |
| TXN-12 | recovered-plan | 6e8ea2d9 | ## TXN-12 | Referral to counsel is a first-class action | §Transactions — referral to counsel | retained | Because the transaction cannot proceed without attorneys on both sides, 'attorney engaged' is a tracked milestone with an owner and a date, and an unrepresented party at deal-sheet stage is a VISIBLE, ACTIONABLE condition rather than a silent gap. PLACEHOLDER_EVIDENCE enumerates the exact tokens that must be rejected: unverified, tbd, pending, todo, n/a, none. | GATE-1, HYG-1, GATE-5 | decided | not_started | source_read |
| GATE-3 | recovered-plan | 6e8ea2d9 | ## GATE-3 | The exit code cannot be asserted | §Completion gate — the exit code cannot be asserted | retained | The gate's honest self-assessment, and it must not be overstated in transfer: it does NOT verify that a recorded exit code is truthful — it cannot. It makes the claim FALSIFIABLE, because command plus target_sha plus environment makes it reproducible by a third party. A false claim becomes a discoverable error rather than an unverifiable opinion. Any restatement claiming the gate verifies truthfulness would be a false claim about the gate itself. | GATE-1, GATE-6 | decided | not_started | source_read |
| GATE-4 | recovered-plan | 6e8ea2d9 | ## GATE-4 | Dependency staleness invalidates evidence | §Completion gate — dependency staleness | retained | When a requirement changes, every completion claim whose evidence predates the change and whose capability depends on it is marked EVIDENCE_STALE and loses promoted status until re-evidenced. This is the enforcement arm of the change protocol: changing a decided requirement does not silently leave downstream capabilities claiming completion against a superseded rule. | GATE-1, DOC-5 | decided | not_started | source_read |
| GATE-5 | recovered-plan | 6e8ea2d9 | ## GATE-5 | Negative findings are first-class | §Completion gate — negative findings are first-class | retained | A search finding nothing is legitimate evidence and must record what was searched, where, and with what pattern. Its `proves` states NOT FOUND IN THE SEARCHED PATHS and may never state DOES NOT EXIST. Carries a concrete operational detail worth preserving: a bare grep finding nothing exits 1, not 0, so where 0 is recorded for a no-match search the wrapper that produced it must be visible in `command`. This ledger's own negative findings (e.g. RESOURCE_NOT_AVAILABLE, acting_capacity) are phrased to this standard. | HYG-1, AGT-6, GATE-2 | decided | not_started | source_read |
| GATE-6 | recovered-plan | 6e8ea2d9 | ## GATE-6 | The gate states its own limits | §Completion gate — the gate states its own limits | retained | It proves every promoted status carries a complete, well-formed, anchored evidence record. It does NOT prove any recorded output is truthful, that any test passes, that anything works in production, or that the capability inventory is complete. 'A green gate means the claims are honest in FORM. Substance is established by the evidence itself and by review.' That sentence should transfer verbatim. | AUD-4, GATE-3 | decided | not_started | source_read |
| GATE-7 | recovered-plan | 6e8ea2d9 | ## GATE-7 | Ratchet | §Completion gate — ratchet | retained | DERIVED from AUD-2. The count of capabilities passing the gate is a baseline and the gate fails if it decreases, so a capability may not be QUIETLY DEMOTED to avoid producing evidence. Without this clause the gate is trivially defeated by lowering a claim instead of evidencing it. | AUD-2 | derived | not_started | source_read |
| GATE-8 | recovered-plan | 6e8ea2d9 | ## GATE-8 | One command | §Completion gate — one command | retained | The gate runs from one named command, reports per-capability results and exits non-zero on any rejection, converging into platform:check (HYG-8). This is the row that connects the completion gate to PR #579's capability-audit machinery, so it must be decided ONCE across both unmerged lines. | HYG-8, PR#579 machine governance | decided | not_started | source_read |
| MKT-1 | recovered-plan | 6e8ea2d9 | ## MKT-1 | Consent model | §Marketing — consent model | retained | Consent is contact BY CHANNEL BY PURPOSE with source, timestamp, evidence and later opt-out. Mallan policy requires affirmative OPT-IN for the applicable channel and purpose and permanently honors later opt-out; both states and their provenance must be durable. Affirmative opt-in is stricter than several legal minimums and is a brokerage-policy choice per BIZ-0. | PER-11, PER-6, MKT-2 | decided | partially_implemented | source_read |
| MKT-2 | recovered-plan | 6e8ea2d9 | ## MKT-2 | Durable opt-out | §Marketing — durable opt-out | retained | Unsubscribe and opt-out are PERMANENT suppression events for the applicable channel and purpose unless a lawful, explicit later change is recorded. PER-11 adds that this survives role changes — a person unsubscribed as a buyer prospect stays unsubscribed when they later become a seller. | PER-11, MKT-1 | decided | partially_implemented | source_read |
| MKT-3 | recovered-plan | 6e8ea2d9 | ## MKT-3 | Contact provenance | §Marketing — contact provenance | retained | Every marketing contact retains source provenance and identity confidence; a PURCHASED OR GUESSED contact may not be silently treated as a consented relationship. Directly a TCPA and CAN-SPAM exposure, and the marketing instance of AGT-6's no-assumption-substitution rule. | PER-6, MKT-1, AGT-6 | decided | not_started | source_read |
| MKT-4 | recovered-plan | 6e8ea2d9 | ## MKT-4 | Production hold | §Marketing — production hold | deferred_with_gate | Source status is DEFERRED, blocked on the consent and provenance data-model decision. Until that model and its enforcement are approved AND PROVED, the system may support classification, suppression, unsubscribe, previews, dry runs and internal test sends only, and may NOT perform live bulk marketing. The documentation clause is the one that binds this reconciliation: 'No documentation, report, or interface may state that consent enforcement has been achieved while this requirement is deferred' — so the canonical plan may not describe consent enforcement as complete. | MKT-1, MKT-3, PER-1 (no Person model) | open | not_started | source_read |
| MKT-5 | recovered-plan | 6e8ea2d9 | ## MKT-5 | Sender separation | §Marketing — sender separation | retained | Transactional and marketing sending must be operationally separable so campaign problems do not impair transaction communication. A marketing-driven reputation or deliverability failure must not prevent a client from receiving a showing confirmation or a deal-sheet notification. | MKT-4 | decided | not_started | source_read |
| MKT-6 | recovered-plan | 6e8ea2d9 | ## MKT-6 | Recipient reconciliation | §Marketing — recipient reconciliation | retained | Nine-line reconciliation before any production campaign (source audience, eligible, suppressed, unsubscribed, invalid, missing consent, policy blocked, duplicates removed, final recipients). THE TOTALS MUST RECONCILE EXACTLY and an unreconciled report BLOCKS THE SEND. Structurally identical to the exclusive object-and-byte reconciliation standard applied to the R2 storage work — every item classified exactly once, totals reconciling exactly, fail closed on any unclassified item. | MKT-4, MKT-7 | decided | not_started | source_read |
| MKT-7 | recovered-plan | 6e8ea2d9 | ## MKT-7 | Content policy | §Marketing — content policy | retained | Marketing content must pass attribution, listing permission, Participant Only, Owner Opt-Out, Fair Housing, unsubscribe and brokerage-policy gates. Note the display gates apply to MARKETING surfaces, not only to public search — BIZ-9's prohibition on compensation in listing content explicitly extends to campaigns. | POL-1, POL-5, BIZ-9, BIZ-8 | decided | not_started | source_read |
| MKT-8 | recovered-plan | 6e8ea2d9 | ## MKT-8 | Existing suppression retained | §Marketing — existing suppression retained | retained | Existing fail-closed suppression behavior is retained UNCHANGED, and any bypass parameter remains restricted to transactional sending and is NEVER enabled for marketing. A no-regression clause: the reconciliation and any later refactor may not weaken suppression that already works. Consistent with the standing instruction not to remove existing safety fallbacks. | MKT-4, MKT-7 | decided | production_proven | source_read |
| INT-1 | recovered-plan | 6e8ea2d9 | ## INT-1 | Intelligence is a consumer, not the owner | §Intelligence — consumer, not owner | retained | AI and analytics CONSUME verified system data; they do not own listing facts, identities, consent, policy or workflow state. The boundary that keeps every compliance gate upstream of the model rather than dependent on it. | INT-3, INT-4 | decided | not_started | source_read |
| INT-2 | recovered-plan | 6e8ea2d9 | ## INT-2 | Explainability | §Intelligence — explainability | retained | Ten attributes per recommendation or signal including evidence, source timestamps, confidence, reason codes, policy status, expiration, assigned person, human approval requirement, and MODEL/PROVIDER/VERSION WHEN AI IS USED. 'No unexplained score is presented as truth.' Model provenance is the AI counterpart of POL-4's policy versioning — without it a recommendation cannot be replayed against the model that produced it. | POL-4, PER-9, INT-3 | decided | not_started | source_read |
| INT-3 | recovered-plan | 6e8ea2d9 | ## INT-3 | No invented facts | §Intelligence — no invented facts | retained | AI MAY draft, summarize, explain, prioritize or suggest. It MAY NOT invent listing facts, legal conclusions, consent, identity or completed actions. Each prohibition maps to a specific exposure: invented listing facts breach COT-11, invented legal conclusions breach TXN-1, invented consent breaches MKT-1, invented completed actions breach OPS-1. The permitted/prohibited split must transfer intact — a blanket prohibition would be unusable and a vague one unenforceable. | COT-11, TXN-1, MKT-1, PER-9 | decided | not_started | source_read |
| INT-4 | recovered-plan | 6e8ea2d9 | ## INT-4 | Replaceable providers | §Intelligence — replaceable providers | retained | AI providers remain replaceable ADAPTERS; core business records and workflows remain company-owned. Same adapter discipline ARC-4 applies to the Cotality provider — no vendor becomes the system of record. | ARC-4, INT-1 | decided | not_started | source_read |
| INT-5 | recovered-plan | 6e8ea2d9 | ## INT-5 | Outcome loop | §Intelligence — outcome loop | retained | Records whether recommendations led to contact, showing, decision, offer, agreement, transaction, referral or no action, WITHOUT REWRITING PRIOR EVIDENCE. The append-only clause matches PER-8's timeline discipline: measuring an outcome must not retroactively edit the recommendation that produced it. | PER-8, INT-2, INT-6 | decided | not_started | source_read |
| INT-6 | recovered-plan | 6e8ea2d9 | ## INT-6 | Cost and usefulness ledger | §Intelligence — cost and usefulness ledger | retained | Each intelligence capability records model cost, human review time, failure rate, revision rate, approval rate and observed outcome; capabilities that do not create measurable value are DISABLED OR REDESIGNED. An explicit kill criterion, which is rare and worth preserving — it prevents accumulating AI features that are never evaluated. | INT-5, INT-2 | decided | not_started | source_read |
| OPS-2 | recovered-plan | 6e8ea2d9 | ## OPS-2 | Evidence standard | §Operations — evidence standard | retained | Seven elements per claim: what was checked, exact command, raw output, exit code, exact commit and environment, what the evidence proves, what it does not prove. 'Three of these are not sufficient.' Identical to CLAUDE.md §F and to the memory/EVIDENCE-STANDARD-2026-07-27.md artefact that PR #579 also carries — so this rule exists on BOTH unmerged lines and must be decided once. | GATE-1, PR#579 evidence standard | decided | not_started | source_read |
| OPS-3 | recovered-plan | 6e8ea2d9 | ## OPS-3 | Test layers | §Operations — test layers | retained | Nine applicable layers from pure contract and unit tests through preview or production smoke tests, health probes and data reconciliation queries. The breadth matters: OPS-024 passed 5,814 unit tests because every fixture fabricated ListingKey, so unit coverage alone cannot establish provider-contract correctness. | OPS-4, OPS-024 | decided | partially_implemented | source_read |
| OPS-4 | recovered-plan | 6e8ea2d9 | ## OPS-4 | Production proof | §Operations — production proof | retained | Production proof includes deployed commit, deployment identity, exact endpoint or workflow, timestamp, observed response or behavior, and relevant logs or queries. 'A source read or a passing unit test never substitutes for a rendering or behavior claim.' This is precisely why TXN-10's FARE render is left unverified in this ledger — no live probe was run. | TXN-10, GATE-2 (SOURCE_READ_FOR_BEHAVIOR) | decided | not_started | source_read |
| OPS-5 | recovered-plan | 6e8ea2d9 | ## OPS-5 | Rollback | §Operations — rollback | retained | Every implementation PR states the rollback procedure AND verifies that rollback does not corrupt data, identity, policy or audit history. The second clause is the one usually skipped and the one OPS-024 exercised for real: recovery required both a Vercel rollback and a revert of main. | OPS-024, CRM-4 | decided | partially_implemented | code_verified |
| OPS-6 | recovered-plan | 6e8ea2d9 | ## OPS-6 | No unrelated files | §Operations — no unrelated files | combined | Each branch and PR contains one bounded capability, diffed against its intended base before push and before merge. | OPS-7 | recovered-plan | 6e8ea2d9 | ## OPS-7 | Maya approval | §Operations — Maya approval | retained | No merge or production release occurs without Maya's approval. | OPS-8 | recovered-plan | 6e8ea2d9 | ## OPS-8 | Health checks | §Operations — health checks | retained | Operational probes proving the affected surface responds correctly are run BEFORE AND AFTER a change, with output recorded. | C-1 | PR#579-plan | 7c15b1d5 | ## C-1 — `audit_events` is not the §8.1 transactional outbox | `audit_events` is not the §8.1 transactional outbox | Events and workflow - audit_events is not the outbox | retained | CONVERGENT FINDING - PR #579 reached this independently of the recovered line, from a different starting point. Independent convergence is corroboration, not duplication, and raises confidence in both. C-1 states that `audit_events` is not the transactional outbox. This is the SAME conclusion as the deep-audit finding AUDIT-WORKFLOW-OUTBOX (no domain-event/outbox/workflow separation; AuditEvent must not be repurposed). Retained as the canonical statement of the rule, with AUDIT-WORKFLOW-OUTBOX as supporting evidence. The reason must transfer: audit history is compliance evidence with its own retention obligations, so coupling workflow retries to it would make an evidentiary record mutable for operational reasons. | AUDIT-WORKFLOW-OUTBOX, PER-8, IAM-9 | decided | not_started | code_verified |
| C-2 | PR#579-plan | 7c15b1d5 | ## C-2 — Cotality consolidates on `lib/idx/`; no parallel integration tree | Cotality consolidates on `lib/idx/`; no parallel integration tree | Architecture - Cotality consolidates on lib/idx/ | retained | CONVERGENT FINDING - PR #579 reached this independently of the recovered line, from a different starting point. Independent convergence is corroboration, not duplication, and raises confidence in both. C-2 requires Cotality integration to consolidate on `lib/idx/` with no parallel integration tree. Complements rather than contradicts AUDIT-IDX-GOD-MODULE: that finding says lib/idx/ concentrates too many CONCERNS (sync.ts 2,419 lines plus media-sync.ts 4,002), while C-2 says the answer is to decompose WITHIN one integration tree, not to grow a second one. Both are needed - without C-2 a decomposition could become a parallel tree, which is the ARC-8 competing-contracts failure. | AUDIT-IDX-GOD-MODULE, ARC-4, ARC-8 | decided | not_started | code_verified |

Independent convergence is corroboration, not duplication, and raises confidence in both. C-3 makes Neon/R2 remediation a HARD DEPENDENCY GATE rather than parallel work. Corroborated by AUDIT-NEON-POOL and by AUDIT-MEDIA-DUPLICATION, which found the same media representable in five places (Listing.media, raw_data, promoted columns, ListingMedia, R2) as an in-flight migration. Retained as a gate: capabilities depending on storage or database headroom may not be promoted before it clears. Neon settings and R2 data are standing Maya-approval holds, so the gate is also a governance boundary. | AUDIT-NEON-POOL, AUDIT-MEDIA-DUPLICATION, Neon/R2 holds | open | not_started | code_verified |

| OPS-024 | safe-main | 04db1b99 | docs/PLATFORM-ISSUE-REGISTRY.md | Phase 1A froze Property ingestion for 4 cycles; rollback + main revert; corrected code unmerged | §Operations — incident register | retained | Production incident 2026-07-29 (PR #587): the two keyset Property streams shipped using the default IDX_PLUS_SELECT_FIELDS, which requests SourceSystemKey — renamed to ListingKey by mapTrestleToPrisma AFTER the fetch — and not ListingKey itself. SourceSystemKey is null feed-wide, the cursor/merge layer reads RAW rows before that rename, so every row was rejected missing_listing_key, both streams froze on the bootstrap epoch and four consecutive cycles processed zero records. Recovered by rollback plus revert of main. Must survive reconciliation intact: both planning lines predate it and neither may overwrite it. | OPS-026 | stage-G-finding | 04db1b99 | app/api/listings/route.ts | Public listing pagination occurs before final display and matched-pair filtering | §6 Search architecture · §1 Current verified system baseline | retained | CONFIRMED BY DIRECT CODE READ at 04db1b99 (safe main), read-only. Public listing pagination occurs BEFORE final display and matched-pair filtering, and the reported total never accounts for either. DB branch order in app/api/listings/route.ts: pagination is applied inside the query (skip: dbSkip, take: dbTake, lines 336-337); the total comes from prisma.listing.count({where: dbWhere}) on the SAME where clause with no display or suppression filtering (line 415); filterDisplayableDbListings then runs on the already-cut page (line 434); preferCrmExclusiveOverIdxDuplicate runs after it (line 441); applyPublicListingPostFilters and an optional open-house filter narrow the page further still. NO REFILL AND NO RECOMPUTATION EXISTS: dbTotal is referenced in exactly three places (lines 332, 590, 593) and flows unmodified into total: dbTotal (590) and hasMore: skip + limit < dbTotal (593), while the actually returned count: annotatedListings.length (589) is computed after all filtering. DECISIVE ASYMMETRY: the live-Cotality fallback branch is ordered the OPPOSITE way and is correct — it filters first, derives totalCount from filtered.length (or odataCount), and only then slices (pageListings = filtered.slice(skip, skip + limit), line 940). The two runtimes therefore disagree on pagination semantics, which is SEA-6's determinism guarantee broken across branches and a concrete instance of AUDIT-SEARCH-DUAL-RUNTIME. NOT PROVEN: no production probe was run, so no claim is made about how many production queries currently span a matched pair across a page boundary, nor about observed user impact. NO TEST asserting total against the displayable count was found in lib/search/__tests__/ or tests/runtime/ — per GATE-5 that is 'not found in the searched paths', NOT 'does not exist'. NO CORRECTION IS INCLUDED IN THIS PLANNING BRANCH; no application code was changed. | SEA-6, SEA-7, SEA-10 criterion 3, LST-1, LST-7, AUDIT-SEARCH-DUAL-RUNTIME | decided | not_started | code_verified |
| CONFLICT-POL-GATE34-PORTAL | stage-G-finding | 04db1b99 | lib/compliance/gates.ts + lib/compliance/dto.ts | Portal gate 3/4 null semantics: current code denies on null, recovered plan says displayable | §11 CRM and portals · §13 Compliance and policy versioning | deferred_with_gate | LEDGER-RESOLVED, POLICY-UNRESOLVED. Current code: null -> DENY (lib/compliance/gates.ts:71 affirmPermission wraps coerceStrictBool; null/undefined/missing all deny). Recovered plan POL-1.1: null -> DISPLAYABLE for gates 3 and 4, block only on explicit false. Affected scope on current evidence is PORTAL DTO/READ PATHS ONLY — dto.ts:364 in sanitizeListingForPortal and dto.ts:185 in sanitizeForPortal; the sync WRITE path was not shown to affirm the raw gate-3 field, and db-to-public-dto.ts:279 affirms the already-computed idx_display_yn aggregate, which is a genuine boolean and correct. NOT PROVEN: whether the recovered rule is correct under the governing provider/compliance contract. TEMPORARY OPERATIONAL RULE: preserve current deployed code behaviour, make no code change — the deployed behaviour is the stricter one, so preserving it cannot cause over-disclosure. DECISION GATE: authoritative REBNY/Cotality/compliance-source verification plus Maya approval. Neither the recovered plan nor the current code has been selected as the permanent target. Full record in the ledger's flagged-conflicts section. | POL-1, POL-1.1, POL-1.3, REB-3, CLAUDE.md §E; Maya approval | open | not_started | code_verified |
| CONFLICT-CAPABILITY-VOCABULARY | stage-L-review | 3be70fa4 | config/capabilities.mjs STATUSES vs canonical plan §16 | Capability maturity vocabulary differs between the enforced registry and the canonical plan | §16 Capability maturity | deferred_with_gate | LEDGER-RESOLVED, POLICY-UNRESOLVED. Found by Codex review at head 3be70fa4, verified by direct read. config/capabilities.mjs exports STATUSES = [discovered, designed, contracted, implemented, shadow_mode, limited_release, production, degraded, deprecated, retired]. The canonical plan §16 states [discovered, designed, schema_only, implemented, integrated, limited_release, production_proven, retiring, retired]. The registry has no schema_only / integrated / production_proven / retiring; the plan has no contracted / shadow_mode / production / degraded / deprecated. The registry is the vocabulary `npm run capability:audit` ACTUALLY ENFORCES, so the audit can pass a registry that contradicts the plan it claims to enforce. CONCRETE INSTANCE: CAP-CANONICAL-PROPERTY is `contracted` in the registry while the plan and §9 describe that capability as schema_only. OBSERVED PROVENANCE (not a scored diagnosis): the registry list originates on the PR #579 line and the §16 list on the recovered line; Stage F reconciled neither against the other, so the divergence reached this head unrecorded. Stated as observed provenance only. AGENTS.md §5 restricts causal-diagnosis language to a registered Hypothesis H-### or an Evidence Score of at least 9 on the same line; no such hypothesis is registered here, so no causal claim is made — only what was observed about where each list came from. ## Identifier retirement map

> DOC-8 is normative: a withdrawn or reassigned requirement KEEPS its identifier, marked RETIRED, with the reason and replacement recorded. An identifier is NEVER reassigned to a different requirement without an entry here. No existing identifier may be reused without this mapping.


The recovered line moved REBNY responsibility out of the BUS family into its own family as REB-1 and shifted BUS-5/6/7 down by one. No requirement was lost, but three identifiers now denote different requirements than they did on the PR #585 line — exactly what DOC-8 forbids doing silently.


| old id | old meaning | new canonical id | new meaning | reason | source commit | destination |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `BUS-5` | REBNY responsibility | `REB-1` | REBNY responsibility | Content moved out of the BUS family into the dedicated REB family. BUS-5 now denotes 'Mallan responsibility'. | `f51848b0 (old) -> 6e8ea2d9 (new)` | §2 Product and business platform -> REB-1 |
| `BUS-6` | Mallan responsibility | `BUS-5` | Mallan responsibility | Downshifted by one when REBNY responsibility left the BUS family. | `f51848b0 (old) -> 6e8ea2d9 (new)` | §2 Product and business platform -> BUS-5 |
| `BUS-7` | Repository boundary | `BUS-6` | Repository boundary | Downshifted by one. NOTE: the identifier BUS-7 was then REUSED on the recovered line for genuinely new content ('No dependency on external listing-entry products') that has no PR #585 counterpart — so BUS-7 is both retired-as-old-meaning and live-as-new-meaning, and this entry is what disambiguates them. | `f51848b0 (old) -> 6e8ea2d9 (new)` | §2 Product and business platform -> BUS-6 |
| `ARC-1` | No client-side MLS calls | `ARC-1` | No client-side provider calls | NOT a reassignment — same requirement, corrected wording. Recorded here so the wording change is traceable and is not mistaken for a reassignment. The constraint binds the Cotality/Trestle provider adapter, not 'MLS' as a category. | `f51848b0 (old) -> 6e8ea2d9 (new)` | §4 Provider contract architecture -> ARC-1 |
| `POL-1` | Compliance fails closed | `POL-1` | Compliance fails closed, except where the feed is pre-filtered | NOT a reassignment — same identifier, SUPERSEDED statement. The unqualified form is the uniform reading that produced the 2026-04-30 incident (7,594 rows suppressed). The qualified form plus POL-1.1..POL-1.5 supersedes it ABSOLUTELY. Recorded so that any artefact citing the PR #585-era POL-1 is known to cite the superseded statement. | `f51848b0 (superseded) -> 6e8ea2d9 (current)` | §13 Compliance and policy versioning -> POL-1, POL-1.1..POL-1.5 |

## Flagged conflicts — OPEN, requiring Maya's decision (1)

> These are conflicts this reconciliation **deliberately did not resolve**.
> REB-3 and CLAUDE.md §E require stopping and reporting when a compliance
> requirement is unclear or conflicting, rather than silently picking one reading.


### CONFLICT-POL-GATE34-PORTAL

**Ledger disposition:** `deferred_with_gate` — Ledger-resolved, POLICY-UNRESOLVED. The row is accounted for; the product and compliance decision is not made.  

**Status:** OPEN — requires authoritative verification plus Maya approval. NOT decided by this reconciliation.  
**Raised by:** Stage F1 batch 5, while transferring POL-1.1; restated to the required shape in Stage G


| aspect | statement |
|---|---|
| **current code behavior** | null -> DENY |
| **recovered-plan behavior** | null -> DISPLAYABLE for gates 3 and 4 |
| **affected scope** | Portal DTO / read paths only, based on current evidence. Both affirming call sites are portal read paths — dto.ts:364 inside sanitizeListingForPortal (defined line 338) and dto.ts:185 inside sanitizeForPortal (defined line 275). The sync WRITE path that computes idx_display_yn was NOT shown to affirm the raw gate-3 field, and db-to-public-dto.ts:279 affirms the already-computed idx_display_yn aggregate, which is a genuine boolean and is correct. |
| **not currently proven** | Whether the recovered rule is correct under the governing provider and compliance contract. Also unproven: whether the write path affirms the raw gate-3 field; whether internet_entire_listing_display_yn is null for a material number of production rows; and whether the regression test POL-1.3 requires exists — no such test was located, which per HYG-1 and GATE-5 is 'not found in the searched paths', NOT 'does not exist'. memory/IDX-PLUS-DISPLAY-GATE-2026-04-30.md was confirmed present but not re-read in this session. |
| **temporary operational rule** | PRESERVE CURRENT DEPLOYED CODE BEHAVIOUR. Make no code change. The deployed behaviour is the stricter one (deny on null), so preserving it cannot cause an over-disclosure; the risk it carries is under-display, which is recoverable, whereas changing it on an unverified reading is not. |
| **decision gate** | Authoritative REBNY / Cotality / compliance-source verification, plus Maya approval. REB-3 and CLAUDE.md §E both require stopping and reporting rather than selecting a reading. |

**Supporting facts verified (read-only, at `04db1b99`):**

- lib/compliance/gates.ts:71 affirmPermission() wraps coerceStrictBool(), true ONLY for explicit true/"true"/"TRUE"; null, undefined and missing all return false (deny).
- lib/compliance/gates.ts docblock lists InternetEntireListingDisplayYN and InternetAddressDisplayYN explicitly under "Use this for".
- lib/compliance/dto.ts:364 comment: "Gate 3 — Internet display (fail-CLOSED). Was `=== false`; null/undefined now also blocks display." — the tightening was deliberate.
- lib/compliance/dto.ts:185 comment records the same deliberate change for gate 4.
- memory/IDX-PLUS-DISPLAY-GATE-2026-04-30.md is present in the repository.

**Obligation on the canonical plan.** The canonical plan MUST NOT state either null behaviour as universally correct. It must describe: (1) current behaviour, (2) the conflicting proposed behaviour, (3) scope, (4) required evidence, (5) decision owner, (6) the prohibition on implementation until resolved.


**Actions deliberately not taken:**

- No code was changed.
- No test was added or removed.
- No production database query was run.
- No claim was made that production is currently non-compliant.
- Neither the recovered plan nor the current code was selected as the permanent target.
